Now the fundamental theorem.

```
Theorem fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma,
    (forall x, lc_at 0 (sigma x)) ->
    (forall x A, Gamma x = Some A -> R A (sigma x)) ->
    R T (subst sigma t).
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T H. induction H as [Gamma x T Hx | L Gamma T1 T2 t1 Hprem IH | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2 | Gamma | Gamma].
  - (* T_Var *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl. apply Hctx. exact Hx.
  - (* T_Abs *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl.
    apply R_abs.
    + (* lc_at 1 (subst sigma t1) *)
      apply (lc_subst sigma Hlc 1 t1).
      apply (lc_open_fvar_inv 0 t1 (fresh L)).
      (* need lc_at 0 (open_rec 0 (fvar (fresh L)) t1), from IH (fresh L) ... *)
      ...
    + (* forall u, R T1 u -> R T2 (open (subst sigma t1) u) *)
      intros u HRu.
      ...
  - (* T_App *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl.
    apply R_app with (A := T1).
    + apply IH1; assumption.
    + apply IH2; assumption.
  - (* T_True *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl. apply R_iff. split.
    + apply lc_true.
    + split; [apply value_sn; constructor | exact I].  (* for Bool, Vclause = True *)
  - (* T_False *) similar.
Qed.
```

      apply (lc_open_fvar_inv 0 t1 (fresh L)).

Let me write the step decomposition lemmas first and test them.

```
Lemma multi_first_step : forall (s v : tm), s -->* v ->
    s = v \/ (exists s', s --> s' /\ s' -->* v).
Proof.
  intros s v H. induction H as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IH].
  - left. reflexivity.
  - right. exists y. split; assumption.
Qed.
```

Now the step_app decomposition. Let me write it and test:
```
Lemma step_app_inv : forall (t u w : tm), tm_app t u --> w ->
  (exists (Tbody : ty) (body : tm),
      t = tm_abs Tbody body /\ value (tm_abs Tbody body) /\ value u /\ w = open body u)
  \/ (exists t', t --> t' /\ w = tm_app t' u)
  \/ (exists u', value t /\ u --> u' /\ w = tm_app t u').
Proof.
  intros t u w H. inversion H; subst.
  - left. exists T, t0. split; [reflexivity | split].
    + apply v_abs. assumption.
    + split; [assumption | reflexivity].
  - right. left. exists t1'. split; [assumption | reflexivity].
  - right. right. exists t2'. split; [assumption | split; [assumption | reflexivity]].
Qed.

Lemma R_app_value : forall (A B : ty) (t u : tm),
    value t ->
    (forall v, t -->* v -> value v ->
       forall (Tbody : ty) (body : tm), v = tm_abs Tbody body ->
         forall w, R A w -> R B (open body w)) ->
    strongly_normalizing u ->
    R A u ->
    R B (tm_app t u).
Proof.

  revert B Hclos HRu.

```
Lemma R_app_aux : forall (A B : ty) (t u : tm),
    lc_at 0 t ->
    strongly_normalizing t ->
    (forall v, t -->* v -> value v ->
       forall (Tbody : ty) (body : tm), v = tm_abs Tbody body ->
         forall w, R A w -> R B (open body w)) ->
    R A u ->
    R B (tm_app t u).
Proof.
  intros A B t u Hlct HSNt Hclos HRu.
  revert B Hclos Hlct.
  induction HSNt as [t0 Hred IH].
  intros B Hclos Hlct.
  assert (Hlcu : lc_at 0 u) by (apply (R_lc A); exact HRu).
  assert (HSNu : strongly_normalizing u) by (apply (R_sn A); exact HRu).
  apply R_iff. split.
  - apply lc_app; assumption.
  - split.
    + apply SN_intro. intros w Hstep.
      destruct (step_app_inv t0 u w Hstep) as
        [[Tbody [body [Heq [Hvabs [Hvalu Hw]]]]]
        | [[t' [Hstep' Hw]] | [u' [Hvalt [Hstep' Hw]]]]].
      * subst t0. subst w.
        apply R_sn with (T := B).
        exact (Hclos (tm_abs Tbody body) (multi_refl step (tm_abs Tbody body))
                     Hvabs Tbody body eq_refl u HRu).
      * subst w.
        apply R_sn with (T := B).
        apply IH.
        -- exact Hstep'.
        -- (* Hclos for t' *)
           intros v Hm Hval Tbody body Heq w0 HRw.
           exact (Hclos v (multi_step step t0 t' v Hstep' Hm) Hval Tbody body Heq w0 HRw).
        -- apply (step_lc t0 t' Hstep' Hlct).
      * subst w.
        apply R_sn with (T := B).
        apply (R_app_value A B t0 u').
        -- exact Hvalt.
        -- exact Hclos.
        -- apply (SN_step u u' HSNu Hstep').
        -- apply (R_step A u u' HRu Hstep').
    + destruct B as [| B1 B2].
      * exact I.
      * simpl. intros v Hm Hvalv Tbody' body' Heqv w0 HRw.
        destruct (multi_first_step (tm_app t0 u) v Hm) as [Heq | [s' [Hstep Hrest]]].
        -- exfalso. apply (app_not_value t0 u). rewrite Heq. exact Hvalv.
        -- destruct (step_app_inv t0 u s' Hstep) as
             [[Tbody [body [Heq [Hvabs [Hvalu Hw]]]]]
             | [[t' [Hstep' Hw]] | [u' [Hvalt [Hstep' Hw]]]]].
           ++ subst t0. subst s'.
              assert (HRs' : R (Ty_Arrow B1 B2) (open body u)).
              { exact (Hclos (tm_abs Tbody body) (multi_refl step (tm_abs Tbody body))
                             Hvabs Tbody body eq_refl u HRu). }
              pose proof (R_multi (Ty_Arrow B1 B2) (open body u) v HRs' Hrest) as HRv.
              exact (R_vclause (Ty_Arrow B1 B2) v HRv v (multi_refl step v) Hvalv Tbody' body' Heqv w0 HRw).
           ++ subst s'. exfalso. exact (value_no_step t0 t' Hvalt Hstep').
           ++ subst s'.
              pose proof (IH t' Hstep' (Ty_Arrow B1 B2)
                               (fun v Hm0 Hval0 Tbody0 body0 Heq0 ww HRw0 =>

R_app_aux and R_app compile! Now the fundamental theorem and the normalization theorem.

Let me write the fundamental theorem carefully.

```
Lemma fundamental : forall (Gamma : context) (t : tm) (T : ty),
    has_type Gamma t T ->
    forall sigma,
      (forall x, lc_at 0 (sigma x)) ->
      (forall x A, Gamma x = Some A -> R A (sigma x)) ->
      R T (subst sigma t).
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T H.
  induction H as [Gamma x T Hx
                 | L Gamma T1 T2 t1 Hprem IH
                 | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
                 | Gamma | Gamma].
  - (* T_Var *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl. apply Hctx. exact Hx.
  - (* T_Abs *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl.
    apply R_abs.
    + (* lc_at 1 (subst sigma t1) *)
      apply (lc_subst sigma Hlc 1 t1).
      apply (lc_open_fvar_inv 0 t1 (fresh L)).
      apply (lc_typing (update Gamma (fresh L) T1) (open t1 (tm_fvar (fresh L))) T2).
      apply Hprem. apply fresh_not_in.
    + intros u HRu.
      set (x := fresh (L ++ fv t1)).
      assert (HxL : ~ In x L) by (subst x; apply fresh_not_in_app_l).
      assert (Hxfv : ~ In x (fv t1)) by (subst x; apply fresh_not_in_app_r).
      assert (Hlc' : forall y, lc_at 0 ((fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then u else sigma y) y)).
      { intro y. destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E.
        - apply (R_lc A u HRu).
        - apply Hlc. }
      assert (Hctx' : forall y A, update Gamma x T1 y = Some A ->
                     R A ((fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then u else sigma y) y)).
      { intros y A0 Hy. destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E.
        - apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y.
          unfold update in Hy. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl in Hy. injection Hy as HA. subst A0.
          simpl. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. exact HRu.
        - apply Hctx with (A := A0). unfold update in Hy. rewrite E in Hy. exact Hy. }
      pose proof (IH x HxL (fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then u else sigma y) Hlc' Hctx') as HRbody.
      rewrite (subst_open_fvar t1 sigma x u 0 (R_lc A u HRu) Hlc Hxfv) in HRbody.
      exact HRbody.
  - (* T_App *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl.
    apply (R_app T1 T2).
    + apply IH1; assumption.
    + apply IH2; assumption.
  - (* T_True *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl.
    apply R_iff. split.
    + apply lc_true.
    + split; [apply value_sn; constructor | exact I].
  - (* T_False *) intros sigma Hlc Hctx. simpl.
    apply R_iff. split.
    + apply lc_false.
    + split; [apply value_sn; constructor | exact I].
Qed.
```
